    SELECT a."Id", a."CreatedAt", a."DecidedById", a."DecisionAt", a."DocumentId", a."RequestDelete", a."RequestRead", a."RequestWrite", a."RequesterId", a."Status"
    FROM "AccessRequests" AS a
    WHERE a."RequesterId" = @__userId_0
    LIMIT @__p_2 OFFSET @__p_1
) AS a0
INNER JOIN "Documents" AS d ON a0."DocumentId" = d."Id"
INNER JOIN "AspNetUsers" AS a1 ON a0."RequesterId" = a1."Id"
LEFT JOIN "AspNetUsers" AS a2 ON a0."DecidedById" = a2."Id"
